    <h2 class="cmp-teaser__title">
        Led by Experience, Committed to Helping
    </h2>

            
    <div class="cmp-teaser__description"><p>Pathward’s commercial finance leadership team represents a host of diverse financial backgrounds and expertise, including traditional banking, commercial lending and commercial finance. They’ve worked as business advisors and in entrepreneurial ventures. They’re also active within the industry and their communities, where they participate in conferences, publish articles and work with young businesses that need financial advice.&nbsp;</p>
</div>

            
    

        </div>

If you do not allow these cookies to be used, either through your browser settings or if you select "No" (Do not sell or share my personal information/Opt-Out) in the toggle above, you will not experience targeted advertising from our partners.</p></li></ul></div><div class="ot-subgrp-cntr"><ul class="ot-subgrps"><li class="ot-subgrp" data-optanongroupid="C0002"><h5>Performance Cookies</h5><div class="ot-tgl-cntr ot-subgrp-tgl"><div class="ot-tgl ot-hide-tgl"><input type="checkbox" name="switch" id="ot-sub-group-id-C0002" data-optanongroupid="C0002" class="cookie-subgroup-handler" aria-label="Performance Cookies" aria-hidden="true" style=""> <label class="ot-switch" for="ot-sub-group-id-C0002"><span class="ot-switch-nob"></span> <span class="ot-label-txt">Switch Label</span></label> <span class="ot-label-status">label</span></div></div><p class="ot-subgrp-desc">These cookies allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. All information these cookies collect is aggregated and therefore anonymous.
